-- FOR MISSION DROPS
-- entry has first to be formatted in notepad++ with the following
-- ctr H : ^ to " (reg exp enabled : begining of each line start with ")
-- ctr H : "     to " (removes the \tab from begining of lines)
-- ctr H : $ to ", (end of line into ",)
-- other stuffs to change before final copy !
-- ctr H : Bane Of to Bane of (remove capital on Of)
-- ctr H : ,000 to 000 and ,500 to 500 (numerical format changed)
-- ctr H : 2X 5000 to 10000 (double credit caches)
-- ctr H : A	", to A", (B and C) for arbitration rotations 
-- expand this list when needed...
-- copy and paste into the MFS table below... add "/", at the end !!!!
-- Call the function formatMFS on a regular page to see the result... 
-- The mission list considered are defined in Module:FormattingTool/missions
-- The result should be on one of my test pages... 
-- User:Croquemorttime/MissionDropFormating
